# Chinese 06 — Band 5–6-oriented professional and written communication

All 1,200 original text cards and synchronized mappings have completed separate ordinary author/editor review, EXECUTOR resolution and one exhaustive final editorial pass. Verified final corrections were applied together. Repository validation and exact-head CI evidence are recorded in the PR.

This text submission implements [execution #37](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/37), [Chinese plan #3, Stage 06](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/3#stage-06) and [roadmap #1](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/1). The unchanged [Chinese guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#66-advanced-development) remains pinned at commit `e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f`, blob `fae450427c762bfe58e8422478323434a71f028b`. Conceptual deck `deck:zh-Hans-CN:06`.

There are exactly 1,200 original reviewed cards and mappings, `zh-06-0001`–`zh-06-1200`, each with a complete target-only Pinyin reading. Accepted Chinese 00–05 contributes 4,000 preserved cards/maps; delivered 00–06 reaches 5,200. Editorial `accepted` rows are review status, not ORCHESTRATOR acceptance. Spanish's 7,600 cards remain project-order dependencies, not Chinese learner knowledge or translation material.

## Allocation and production/recognition scope

Counters below use `zh-06-` unless another stage is stated.

| Block | Counters | Cards | Actual development |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- |
| A | 0001–0240 | 240 | Professional and abstract lexical senses across work, education/research, services, community and intellectual discussion |
| B | 0241–0430 | 190 | Formal written constructions and original written/spoken alternatives preserving agency, stance and commitment |
| C | 0431–0610 | 180 | Abstract argument, qualification, evidence, conditions and bounded source-based conclusions |
| D | 0611–0780 | 170 | Situated disagreement, indirect requests/criticism, negotiation, actual authority and response status |
| E | 0781–0940 | 160 | Precise event, complement, result/potential, topic, omission and professional collocation extensions beyond 05 |
| F | 0941–1080 | 140 | Contemporary idioms/four-character expressions and collocations; attributed marked alternatives |
| G | 1081–1200 | 120 | Cohesion, conversational particles, additional useful senses and meaning-preserving audience editing |
| Total | 0001–1200 | 1,200 | Shared §6.6 part B |

**1,192 P / eight R.** F contains 132 P and the eight R IDs `0999`, `1000`, `1039`, `1040`, `1077`–`1080`. All other blocks are P. A–E and G require P: contemporary standard Chinese in Simplified production. F alone permits R for marked input, with the actual attributed wording and audience on the front and a natural Simplified interpretation as the hidden unit. Each R mapping explicitly states `Source/register:` and substantive attribution. Formal writing alone is not R; there is no numeric recognition quota. Accepted 00–05 remains P-only. No regional/Traditional production is introduced.

## Complete shared 05/06 §6.6 reconciliation

The following maps every inventory item in the pinned §6.6 to actual hidden retrieval. Visible terminology alone does not count. Accepted 05 remains unchanged; its accepted range descriptions are retained as provenance rather than reaudited content. This completes the project-assigned 05/06 milestone, not an official syllabus, CEFR equivalence or the later ten-stage language reconciliation.

| Required §6.6 item | Accepted 05 hidden development | New 06 hidden development and limits |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Precise aspect choice | `zh-05-0221`–`0228` event framing; `0321`–`0340` repetition/temporal scope | `0783` prospective completion 过, `0784` counterfactual consequent 了, `0785` earlier-knowledge counterfactual and `0786` concessive actual completion. Whole temporal `0781` 正值 and lexical `0782` 不曾 are honestly distinguished from new aspect concepts. |
| Advanced complements | `zh-05-0229`–`0320`, `0341`–`0380`: realized/potential, literal/extended result/direction/degree | `0841`–`0880` new outcome, continuation, assignment, classification and settlement collocations; `0893`–`0908` abstract potential/directional extensions with actual old physical contrasts, plus `0912`–`0940` completion and precise report distinctions. Old grammar with a changed workplace noun is not counted as new. |
| Natural topic management | `zh-05-0381`–`0396`, `0405`–`0432`, `0501`–`0530` topic/turn movement | `0846` 收束 closes an actual two-constraint discussion and `0857` 绕回 returns through actual supplied turns; `0905` 引出 and G attention/perspective prefacing. No invented intervening source or decision. |
| Ellipsis and omission | `zh-05-0405`–`0432`, `0440`–`0460`: recoverable actors, objects and reference | `0931` 各自的 focuses recoverable headless possession with two explicit owners and notebook pairings. `0816` 历经 is lexical event progression; its visible actor omission is not counted as a new hidden omission lesson. G applies recoverability in actual shortening (`1166`) and ownership/reference clarification (`1171`, `1189`); these editing lexical counterparts do not independently claim to introduce a new ellipsis grammar system. `0827` 所 is a newly focused passive component inside the already known 为…所… frame, not new headless coverage or a newly introduced passive construction. |
| Cohesive devices | `zh-05-0433`–`0460`, `0479`–`0530`, F narrative/reformulation | `0431`–`0610` source-bound positions/qualification/relations; `0828` 前述, `0829` 下述, `0830` 其一 and `0831` 二者 retrieve actual reference/organization; G `1093`–`1095` retrieves reverse perspective and summary markers with actual original material; `1099`–`1100` focuses evidence-attention and familiar attention-prefacing, while `1183` applies known sequence links in a connected-account lexical lesson. |
| Formal written constructions | `zh-05-0901`–`0912` limited contemporary written bridge | `0241`–`0430` original paired spoken/written forms; `0791`–`0800` bounded negation/modality/procedural framing; `0802`–`0815` written event/status framing and `0817`–`0832` scope/reference. No new institutional rule or asserted approval from a formula. |
| Conversational particles | `zh-05-0661`–`0680`, later E stance/interaction | `0788` scalar 都, `0790` argumentative 又 and `0784` counterfactual 了; `1081`–`1085` distinct situated interjection uses/readings, `1086`–`1090` stance/interaction and `1101`–`1106` rhetorical/relief frames. No imagined prosody or listening/native-human verification. |
| Four-character expressions used naturally | `zh-05-0531`–`0660` idioms and productive/conventional expressions | `0941`–`1080`, including ordinary professional collocations, bounded evaluative accounts and context-specific marked alternatives. Four characters do not automatically make a chengyu. |
| Common idioms | `zh-05-0531`–`0660` contemporary interaction, learning, conduct and outcomes | F idioms in original concrete professional/written situations, with actual roles, source facts and bounded evaluation. Origins omitted unless separately verified; dictionary examples are not sentence material. |
| Professional terminology | 05 lexical breadth and limited written bridge support 06 | `0001`–`0240`: roles/coordination, research/learning, abstract concepts, services/resources and community/public communication. B/C/E add actual register, explanation and result frames, not a translated terminology glossary. |
| Rhetorical structures | `zh-05-0791`–`0900` narrative/nonfiction comparison, summary and reformulation | `0431`–`0610` premises, evidence, necessary/sufficient conditions, concession, alternatives and source-bounded conclusions; `1093`–`1105` reverse perspective, source-preserving summaries and bounded rhetorical questions; `1161`–`1200` develops source-based editing with stated purpose and deliberately identified incidental omissions. Cause differs from sequence and proof from support. |
| Written/spoken differences | `zh-05-0901`–`0912` brief written/spoken bridge | `0241`–`0430` complete actual formulations; F marked-input interpretations and `1161`–`1200` actual source-based editing. `1174` compares direct request wording, `1184`/`1185` retrieve conversational/written adaptation and `1191` plain-language explanation. These are lexical actions/assessments with inspectable short originals, not forty newly claimed grammar systems. Actors, numbers, time, modality, attribution and commitments remain material. |
| Polite disagreement | `zh-05-0761`–`0790` reservation, mitigation and disagreement | `0611`–`0780`, including `0692` conventional 不敢苟同, with actual preceding words, roles, authority/familiarity and purpose; answer/decision may remain pending. Lexical counterparts do not invent new universal politeness concepts. |
| Indirect requests and criticism | 05 E courtesy/turn management and F reformulation | `0611`–`0780`: bounded favors, payment/time negotiation, refusal, face-saving and criticism with speaker-owned intent and actual or pending response. A request is not instruction, acknowledgment not assent, silence not consent and an offer not fulfillment. |

EXECUTOR has checked this fourteen-item reconciliation against the actual completed hidden units and maps and retained accepted 05 evidence. It accounts for all seven 06 allocations and the actual F-only P/R outcome. After the exhaustive final pass and verified corrections, no unresolved teachable omission has been identified in the assigned shared milestone. Later advanced source precision, broader literary/cultural mediation and Traditional/regional recognition remain reserved for 07–08; 07–09 and the final ten-stage reconciliation are not completed here.

## Review, progression and reference limits

New hidden learning was compared with the complete accepted 4,000-card Chinese hidden inventory and current 06 inventory, using actual relevant complete predecessor records and all ten mapping cells. Repeated strings are allowed for defensible new senses/frames, and new lexical counterparts can share known concepts. Mere visible exposure, accidental cross-word substrings, a changed gloss or a workplace noun do not create a new lesson. Concrete duplicate objectives were repaired within their stable block IDs before authoring or ingestion, and actual source/voice/status findings were resolved.

Every child uses the unchanged verified `gpt-6.1-sol` CLI environment, high reasoning, separate fresh ephemeral read-only processes and structured JSON, without tools/nested agents/repository edits. EXECUTOR reads outputs, resolves limitations and writes accepted records/maps. Ordinary review used 30 sequential fresh 40-card author/editor pairs for `0001`–`1200` (60 processes), plus one focused editor for actual blocking novelty repair `0511` (zero further findings). EXECUTOR resolved all 298 ordinary editor findings. The single exhaustive final pass covered consecutive ranges `0001–0400`, `0401–0800`, `0801–1200`: every complete source field, actual optional presence and all ten mapping cells, with the complete current 1,200-card inventory, reconciliation and relevant complete outside support. EXECUTOR resolved all 36 final findings and applied verified corrections jointly to 37 records. Actual process total: 64, comprising 60 ordinary, one focused and 3 final processes. All actual CLI completions were zero with normal turn/structured-result/empty-stderr evidence; 63 outer-wrapper zero receipts were observed, with the one disclosed batch-12 gap. Every thread ID is distinct; no process was resumed or replayed. Ordinary ingestion used scoped new/changed-record checks. The one final pass preceded joint corrections, focused implementation tests and one consolidated validation. Actual maximum prompt-plus-schema input was 1,024,906 characters and 1,024,906 UTF-16 units, below the retained 1,048,576 limit. Complete fields, optional presence and all map cells were preserved with checked lossless transport; final ranges were not sampled or repeated. Detailed per-process and CI evidence belongs in the PR.

Whole-unit Pinyin/sense decisions distinguish actual opened primary passages, indexed selected excerpts and failed reads. Useful connected-speech changes are separate from selected lexical tones; supported full/neutral/spacing variants are not automatically errors. Examples remain original; no bulk dictionary/guide/corpus/examination sentence bank or automatic converter is used. Primary sources do not establish independent native-human review or listening. Actual scoped decisions and access limitations are retained in the PR evidence. G uses distinct supported questioning ńg/ó/á, same-reading hāi with a new sigh function and standalone laughter hā. Whole 订明 dìngmíng and 誊写 téngxiě have actually opened HanDian entries. The corrected object-marking 拿 in 拿A当B has an actually opened publisher definition with that complete frame, rather than a character-only reading check. Indexed-only questioning 啊 and university 回扣 usage retain their access limits.

One evidence limitation remains: the batch-12 editor's outer tool-wrapper completion receipt was lost after a user-reported tool-server restart. Its durable CLI exit 0, normal turn completion, matching structured result and empty stderr were independently inspected; the original process was not replayed. This is not an observed outer-wrapper zero.

## Reproducible source-string measures

| Source-string measure | Chinese 06 | Chinese 00–06 cumulative |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Cards / mappings | 1,200 | 5,200 |
| Distinct NFC exact cloze strings | 1,198 | 4,891 |
| Distinct NFC exact lemma strings | 1,198 | 4,885 |
| Operational exact (lemma, meaning) pairs | 1,200 | 5,199 |
| D: hidden-cloze Han | 917 | 1,821 |
| S: whole-sentence Han | 1,470 | 2,169 |
| V: visible-front Han | 1,252 | 1,746 |
| I: sentence Han outside hidden set (S − D) | 553 | 348 |

Measures were computed from the fixed final editorial source after joint corrections. Distinct NFC `cloze`, `lemma` and exact (`lemma`, `meaning`) pairs are source-string measures, not semantic segmentation, mastered words or independent concepts.

Han sets use U+3400–U+4DBF and U+4E00–U+9FFF only. D is Han in hidden clozes; S is Han in complete sentences; V removes each sentence's own single cloze once before taking the union; I = S − D. V may overlap D. On R cards, the attributed marked input is visible and contributes to S/V; its Han enters D only if it also occurs in a hidden cloze. These source-position sets do not decide whether that visible recognition input is pedagogically targeted or incidentally encountered. English, Pinyin and back-side notes are excluded. Columns use 06 and actual accepted 00–05 plus 06 (5,200), without future stages.

The shared Band 5–6 working landmarks remain at least 1,800 recognized characters, approximately 5,500–7,000 words/phrases and approximately 700 handwritten characters. These are distinct from card/source-string counts and from the guide's cited Band 6 standard anchor (1,800 characters/5,456 words/phrases). No newly verified official inventory, CEFR/HSK equivalence, handwriting, listening, attained vocabulary or completed ten-stage language reconciliation is claimed. Chinese 07–09 and all audio remain later assignments.
